<!-- ── Research ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<section class="section research-immersive" id="research">
  <canvas id="researchCanvas" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>

  <div class="container">
    <div class="section-head fade-up">
      <div>
        <p class="section-num" data-i18n-html="sec.01.label">02 &mdash; Research</p>
        <h2 class="section-title display" data-i18n="sec.01.title">Areas of Focus</h2>
      </div>
      <p class="section-head-right" data-i18n-html="sec.01.desc">
        From experimental 300&thinsp;GHz THz links to neuromorphic photonics.
        Building the physical layer of 6G.
      </p>
    </div>
  </div>

  <div class="research-panels">

    <div class="rp" data-rp="0">
      <div class="rp-visual">
        <img class="rp-img" src="images/THz_PN.jpg" alt="" aria-hidden="true" />
      </div>
      <div class="rp-body container">
        <span class="rp-num" aria-hidden="true">01</span>
        <div class="rp-content">
          <h3 data-i18n-html="research.1.title">Terahertz Wireless<br />Communications</h3>
          <p data-i18n-html="research.1.text">Experimental 300&thinsp;GHz (WR-3) transmission systems built on photonic upconversion, from PIN-photodiode transmitters to receiver-side DSP. Demonstrated throughputs beyond 200&thinsp;Gbit/s, with a record <strong>221&thinsp;Gb/s dual-polarisation</strong> link over 500&thinsp;m.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="rp rp--flip" data-rp="1">
      <div class="rp-visual">
        <img class="rp-img" src="images/pcsdark.png" alt="" aria-hidden="true" style="object-position: center 65%;" />
      </div>
      <div class="rp-body container">
        <span class="rp-num" aria-hidden="true">02</span>
        <div class="rp-content">
          <h3 data-i18n-html="research.2.title">Adaptive Modulation &amp;<br />Converged 6G Links</h3>
          <p data-i18n-html="research.2.text">Rate-adaptive probabilistic shaping approaching the Shannon limit, integrated into converged fiber&ndash;THz backhaul architectures for 6G. The subject of my PhD dissertation at TU Berlin.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="rp" data-rp="2">
      <div class="rp-visual">
        <img class="rp-img" src="images/neuromorphic.png" alt="" aria-hidden="true" />
      </div>
      <div class="rp-body container">
        <span class="rp-num" aria-hidden="true">03</span>
        <div class="rp-content">
          <h3 data-i18n-html="research.3.title">Neuromorphic<br />Photonic Computing</h3>
          <p data-i18n-html="research.3.text">Optical signal processing on integrated photonic chips using neural networks: modulation format identification, signal correction, and feature extraction, entirely in the optical domain at sub-nanosecond latency.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

  </div>
</section>

<!-- ── Venture ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── -->
<section class="section venture-section" id="venture">
  <div class="container">
    <div class="section-head fade-up">
      <div>
        <p class="section-num" data-i18n-html="sec.venture.label">03 &mdash; Venture</p>
        <h2 class="section-title display" data-i18n="sec.venture.title">Building LUXONNIC</h2>
      </div>
      <p class="section-head-right" data-i18n-html="sec.venture.desc">
        Co-Founder &amp; CEO of an early-stage deep-tech venture, developed at Fraunhofer HHI, working to reinvent the optical transceiver for AI data centers.
      </p>
    </div>

    <div class="venture-card fade-up delay-1">
      <div class="venture-card-header">
        <a href="https://www.luxonnic.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="venture-wordmark">LUXONNIC</a>
        <span class="venture-role-badge" data-i18n="venture.role">Co-Founder & CEO</span>
      </div>
      <p class="venture-tagline" data-i18n-html="venture.tagline">&ldquo;Becoming the secret sauce of next-gen transceivers.&rdquo;</p>
      <div class="venture-stats">
        <div class="venture-stat">
          <div class="venture-stat-num">&approx;10%</div>
          <div class="venture-stat-label" data-i18n="venture.stat1.label">of an AI factory’s power goes to optical transceivers</div>
        </div>
        <div class="venture-stat">
          <div class="venture-stat-num">40&thinsp;MW</div>
          <div class="venture-stat-label" data-i18n="venture.stat2.label">per site, mostly spent on digital signal correction</div>
        </div>
        <div class="venture-stat">
          <div class="venture-stat-num">30&times;</div>
          <div class="venture-stat-label" data-i18n="venture.stat3.label">target bit-per-Joule efficiency gain</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <p class="venture-desc" data-i18n-html="venture.desc">
        <strong>LUXONNIC</strong> is developing the <strong>LUXONNICore</strong>, a photonic integrated circuit that performs signal equalization directly in the optical domain using photonic neural networks, replacing the power-hungry DSP chips inside next-generation (LPO/CPO) transceivers.
      </p>
      <div class="pill-row venture-pills">
        <span class="pill">Photonic Neural Networks</span>
        <span class="pill">DSP-Free Optical Interconnects</span>
        <span class="pill">AI Data Center Transceivers</span>
        <span class="pill">6G Core Infrastructure</span>
      </div>
      <p class="venture-meta" data-i18n-html="venture.meta">
        In development at <strong>Fraunhofer HHI</strong>, Department of Photonic Networks and Systems. A funding proposal has been submitted to the <strong>BMFTR xG-Incubator</strong> programme (StartUpConnect).
      </p>
      <a href="https://www.luxonnic.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener" class="venture-link">
        <span data-i18n="venture.visit">Visit luxonnic.com</span> &nbsp;&#8594;
      </a>
    </div>
  </div>
</section>
